Load JSON values\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\nwith open('/root/project_proposal.json', 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n    data = json.load(f)\n\n# Normalize budget: strip commas from numeric part, keep leading ₩\nbudget = data.get('예산', '')\nif budget.startswith('₩'):\n    data['예산'] = '₩' + budget[1:].replace(',', '')\nelse:\n    data['예산'] = budget.replace(',', '')\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# 2. Namespace handling\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\nns_hp = 'http://www.hancom.co.kr/hwpml/2010/HWPML'\n\n# Register namespace so ET doesn't rewrite prefixes\nET.register_namespace('hp', ns_hp)\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# 3. Process each XML content file\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\nextracted_dir = '/tmp/hwpx_work/extracted'\ncontents_dir = os.path.join(extracted_dir, 'Contents')\n\nfor xml_name in ('section0.xml', 'section1.xml'):\n    xml_path = os.path.join(contents_dir, xml_name)\n    if not os.path.exists(xml_path):\n        continue\n\n    tree = ET.parse(xml_path)\n    root = tree.getroot()\n\n    modified_any = False\n\n    for para in root.findall(f'{{{ns_hp}}}p'):\n        # Merge all <hp:t> text nodes in this paragraph\n        t_nodes = para.findall(f'.//{{{ns_hp}}}t')\n        if not t_nodes:\n            continue\n\n        merged_text = ''.join(t.text or '' for t in t_nodes)\n        original_text = merged_text\n\n        # -------------------------------------------------------------------\n        # Replace placeholders {{key}}\n        # -------------------------------------------------------------------\n        def replacer(match):\n            key = match.group(1)\n            return data.get(key, match.group(0))\n\n        new_text = re.sub(r'\\{\\{([^}]+)\\}\\}', replacer, merged_text)\n\n        # -------------------------------------------------------------------\n        # Append month spans for phase lines\n        # -------------------------------------------------------------------\n        phase_match = None\n        if '단계1' in new_text or '단계2' in new_text or '단계3' in new_text:\n            # Look for date range patterns like 2026-08 ~ 2026-10, 2026.08~2026.10, 2027-01, etc.\n            # General pattern: YYYY-MM ~ YYYY-MM or YYYY.MM ~ YYYY.MM or YYYY-MM-YYYY-MM etc.\n            # The JSON values are:\n            # 단계1: \"요구사항 분석 및 설계 (2026-08 ~ 2026-10)\"\n            # 단계2: \"시스템 개발 및 테스트 (2026-10 ~ 2026-12)\"\n            # 단계3: \"파일럿 운영 및 안정화 (2027-01)\"\n            range_patterns = [\n                r'(\\d{4})[\\.-](\\d{2})\\s*[~～~]\\s*(\\d{4})[\\.-](\\d{2})',\n                r'(\\d{4})[\\.-](\\d{2})',\n            ]\n            for rp in range_patterns:\n                m = re.search(rp, new_text)\n                if m:\n                    groups = m.groups()\n                    if len(groups) == 4:\n                        start_year, start_month, end_year, end_month = map(int, groups)\n                        span = (end_year - start_year) * 12 + (end_month - start_month) + 1\n                        new_text += f' ({span}개월)'\n                        break\n                    elif len(groups) == 2:\n                        # Single month case (단계3)\n                        new_text += ' (1개월)'\n                        break\n\n        if new_text != original_text:\n            modified_any = True\n\n            # Rebuild paragraph run/text nodes\n            # Set first <hp:t> to the full new text\n            first_t = t_nodes[0]\n            first_t.text = new_text\n\n            # Remove all subsequent <hp:t> nodes (and their parent <hp:run> if empty)\n            for extra_t in t_nodes[1:]:\n                parent_run = extra_t.getparent() if hasattr(extra_t, 'getparent') else None\n                if parent_run is not None:\n                    parent_run.remove(extra_t)\n                    # If run now has no children, remove it\n                    if len(parent_run) == 0:\n                        para.remove(parent_run)\n                else:\n                    # Fallback: search for the element directly in para\n                    for run in para.findall(f'{{{ns_hp}}}run'):\n                        if extra_t in run:\n                            run.remove(extra_t)\n                            if len(run) == 0:\n                                para.remove(run)\n                            break\n\n            # Remove stale layout cache: <hp:lineSegArray> and <hp:lineSeg>\n            for ls in para.findall(f'{{{ns_hp}}}lineSegArray'):\n                para.remove(ls)\n            for ls in para.findall(f'{{{ns_hp}}}lineSeg'):\n                para.remove(ls)\n\n    # start_year) * 12 + (end_month - start_month) + 1\n                new_text += f' ({span}개월)'\n            else:\n                single_match = re.search(r'(\\d{4})[\\.-](\\d{2})', new_text)\n                if single_match:\n                    new_text += ' (1개월)'\n\n        if new_text == original_text:\n            continue\n\n        modified_any = True\n\n        # Rebuild paragraph run/text nodes:\n        # Set first <hp:t> to the full new text, remove all others.\n        first_t = t_nodes[0]\n        first_t.text = new_text\n\n        # Remove extra <hp:t> nodes and their parent <hp:run> if it becomes empty.\n        # Since ET has no getparent(), iterate runs in the paragraph.\n        for run in list(para.findall(f'{{{ns_hp}}}run')):\n            for t_node in list(run.findall(f'{{{ns_hp}}}t')):\n                if t_node is not first_t:\n                    run.remove(t_node)\n            # If the run is now empty, remove it from the paragraph\n            if len(run) == 0:\n                para.remove(run)\n\n        # Remove stale layout cache: <hp:lineSegArray>, <hp:linesegarray>, <hp:lineSeg>, <hp:lineseg>\n        remove_elements_by_tag(para, ns_hp, 'linesegarray')\n        remove_elements_by_tag(para, ns_hp, 'lineseg')\n\n    # Write back preserving XML declaration\n    if modified_any:\n        tree.write(xml_path, encoding='utf-8', xml_declaration=True)\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# 4.
